90) ONOWN0
Computed variable representing whether R ever lived away from parents or guardians for four months or longer. If R has no household members who are natural or adoptive parent, step-parent, foster parent, or guardian (AH-6 RELAR00-RELAR11 NE 9, 10, 11, 12, or 14) and gave a date that she first lived away from parents or guardians (AJ-2 FSTONOWN NE 11197), then ONOWN = YES. If R never lived away from parents or guardians for four months or longer (AJ-1 ONOWNX=2) or responded "never lived on own" to question asking date of first living on own (AJ-2 FSTONOWN=1197), then ONOWN = NO.
